   238  class VisibleTests(unittest.TestCase):
   239      def setUp(self) -> None:
   240          self.scratch = 
Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="migrakit-visible-"))
   241          write_contracts(self.scratch / "contracts")
   242          self.registry = migrakit.load_contracts(self.scratch / 
"contracts")
   243
   244      def test_one_edge_plan(self) -> None:
   245          self.assertEqual(migrakit.plan_migration(self.registry, 
"ledger.v2", {"amount": "4"}), ["L02"])
   246
   247      def test_rename_then_cast_execution(self) -> None:
   248          plan = migrakit.plan_migration(self.registry, "ledger.v1", 
{"amt": "12"})
   249          result = migrakit.execute_plan(self.registry, "ledger.v1", 
{"amt": "12"}, plan, [])
   250          self.assertEqual(result["status"], "migrated")
   251          self.assertEqual(result["payload"], {"amount": 12})
   252
   253      def test_signature_without_nulls(self) -> None:
   254          signature = migrakit.state_signature(self.registry, "ledger.v1",
{"amt": 3, "memo": "x"})
   255          self.assertEqual(signature, {"amt": "value"})
   256
   257      def test_single_hop_alias(self) -> None:
   258          self.assertEqual(migrakit.resolve_partner(self.registry, 
"south-books"), "P002")
   259
   260      def test_unknown_schema_code(self) -> None:
   261          with self.assertRaises(migrakit.MigrationError) as caught:
   262              migrakit.plan_migration(self.registry, "ledger.v9", {})
   263          self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "E_UNKNOWN_SCHEMA")
   264
   265      def test_empty_plan_at_target(self) -> None:
   266          self.assertEqual(migrakit.plan_migration(self.registry, 
"ledger.v3", {"amount": 1}), [])
   267
   268      def test_fingerprint_stable(self) -> None:
   269          first = migrakit.record_fingerprint("ledger.v1", {"b": 1, "a": 
[1, 2]})
   270          second = migrakit.record_fingerprint("ledger.v1", {"a": [1, 2], 
"b": 1})
   271          self.assertEqual(first, second)
   272          self.assertEqual(len(first), 64)
   273
   274      def test_module_entry_help(self) -> None:
   275          env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(ROOT / "src"))
   276          done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "migrakit", 
"--help"], capture_output=True, text=True, env=env, cwd=self.scratch)
   277          self.assertEqual(done.returncode, 0, done.stderr)
   278
   279      def test_cli_runs_from_other_folder(self) -> None:
   280          feed = self.scratch / "feed.jsonl"
   281          feed.write_text(
   282              json.dumps({"batch": "B1", "partner": "north-books", "key": 
"k1", "schema": "ledger.v1", "payload": {"amt": "5"}}) + "\n",
   283              encoding="utf-8",
   284          )
   285          (self.scratch / "corrections.txt").write_text("# none\n", 
encoding="utf-8")
   286          (self.scratch / 
"manifest.csv").write_text("batch_id,received_seq,lines,fp_xor\nB1,1,1,db4e5a9d\
n", encoding="utf-8")
   287          env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(ROOT / "src"))
   288          script = "import sys; from migrakit.cli import main; 
sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))"
   289          args = ["run", "--contracts", "contracts", "--feed", 
"feed.jsonl", "--corrections", "corrections.txt", "--manifest", "manifest.csv", 
"--out", "out"]
   290          done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", script, *args], 
capture_output=True, text=True, env=env, cwd=self.scratch)
   291          self.assertEqual(done.returncode, 0, done.stderr)
   292          record = json.loads((self.scratch / "out" / 
"records.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   293          self.assertEqual(record["status"], "migrated")
   294          self.assertEqual(record["payload"], {"amount": 5})

requires following until a fixpoint with cycle detection raising E_ALIAS_CYCLE —

partner-id matches still take priority over aliases.

In records.py I'm aligning null handling and fill-mode fallbacks with the 
registry defaults, and reconciling a README statement that contradicts the 
actual null-handling contract by updating the docs rather than the behavior. I'm
now moving into operations.py to look at the rename logic.

I'm checking the add, cast, and convert operations: rename moves a field and 
replaces the target only when the source is present; add leaves values alone 
when there's no default, applying the fixed fill-mode logic; cast only 
transforms values that are actually present, leaving nulls and absent fields 
untouched.

Now I'm working through the type-conversion rules for int, float, and string — 
checking that bools short-circuit before numeric coercion, that integral floats 
convert to int, signed digit strings parse correctly, and that decimal strings 
convert to float — while flagging a potential edge case where the digit-matching
regex might catch non-ASCII digits that `float()` wouldn't otherwise accept.

I'm also questioning whether the decimal regex should accept strings like ".5" 
or "5." given the spec's phrasing about "digits with an optional point," and I 
want to check the actual feed/corrections data to see if such edge-case strings 
even appear before worrying further. Separately, I'm verifying that 
`apply_operations` only does a shallow copy of the record and confirming that 
rename just moves references without mutating nested values.

Moving to planner.py, I'm rewriting it to run Dijkstra over schema/state pairs 
with lexicographic tiebreaking and no visited-set restriction, checking 
prerequisites against the current state at each step. I'm confirming 
state_signature correctly uses FieldView.from_payload with fixed-to-null states 
and prerequisite fields, satisfying K5/M10, and that it raises E_UNKNOWN_SCHEMA 
through domain_of as required, then moving on to plan_migration's error handling
for E_UNKNOWN_SCHEMA and E_UNREACHABLE per K11.

I'm also checking that the planner operates strictly on the payload as given 
with no token substitution (K6), tracking named-field states for edge-allowed 
checks using the current view. Estimating complexity: with named fields capped 
at 10, each schema has up to 59049 states, and across roughly a dozen domain 
schemas with ~6000 total edges the search space seems large but likely tractable
since operations are deterministic, so I plan to implement it with best-label 
memoization to skip stale entries and then measure actual performance.

I'm settling on Dijkstra using a heap of (cost, path, node, state) tuples, 
storing full paths for the lexicographic tie-break since memory isn't a concern,
and relying on standard dominance pruning where finalized states are skipped on 
repeat pops.

The lexicographic ordering argument holds because equal-cost paths can't have 
one be a prefix of the other (positive edge costs prevent that), so the first 
differing index determines ordering and that ordering is preserved under suffix 
extension. Since Dijkstra pops in strictly increasing key order, the first time 
the target node is popped (in any state) gives the globally optimal label, so I 
can safely terminate there.

Now I'm turning to executor.py: the plan is to deepcopy the payload, group 
corrections by their pinned schema in log order, and simulate arrivals — 
starting at the source schema, then at each subsequent schema after traversing 
an edge. Only the first arrival at a given schema triggers application of its 
pinned corrections; I need to track which schemas have already been visited so 
later arrivals are no-ops.

Then at each edge, I check prerequisites before applying operations and 
advancing — and I'm reasoning through whether the final arrival at the canonical
schema should also trigger its corrections, concluding that since there's no 
next edge to gate against, arrival still counts and corrections pinned there 
should apply to the migrated payload.

For the empty plan case where the record is already canonical, arrival at the 
source schema still triggers its pinned corrections. Corrections pinned to 
schemas never visited in the execution don't get applied, and if execution fails
partway, only corrections up to the failure point count — I also need to track 
`corrections_applied` as an ordered list, and on failure emit `E_PREREQ_FAILED` 
with null payload, the schema reached, completed step count, and the failed edge
id.

Moving to `execute_plan`, I need it to look up the domain for operations via 
`registry.domain_of(schema_id)`, which raises `E_UNKNOWN_SCHEMA` if unrecognized
— that seems reasonable even though it's not explicitly listed.

input get caught as a parse problem rather than an uncaught exception, and 
reviewing the shape validation logic for missing or empty keys and non-object 
payloads — including the edge case where the payload key is absent entirely 
versus present but invalid.

Then I'm examining `_blank_record` in the pipeline: for parse errors, 
batch/partner/key/schema should all be null per spec, and the pipeline does pass
None for document in that case, so that checks out, as does the 
manifest-missing-batch case which also nulls everything out. For admitted 
records, `_blank_record` correctly populates batch/key/schema from the document.

For corrections.py, I'm verifying the line-splitting logic for SET, UNSET/NULL, 
and REISSUE commands — checking expected token counts (8 for SET with the value 
being the remainder of the line, 7 exact for UNSET/NULL, 5 for REISSUE with a 
12-hex-char prefix) and flagging a possible issue where UNSET's split could 
absorb extra tokens into the last field, which affects the length check.

Now I'm checking the malformed-id extraction: `parse_corrections` strips and 
splits each line, grabbing `line.split()[0]` as the id even if the line has

valid id in that fallback case.

I'm verifying the fingerprint prefix regex matches exactly 12 lowercase hex 
digits, and double-checking that operation names must match 
"SET"/"UNSET"/"NULL"/"REISSUE" exactly or the parser returns None.

I'm noting that json.loads could technically parse "NaN" as valid float which 
would break downstream JSON output, though this likely doesn't occur in real 
data, while malformed trailing content correctly raises JSONDecodeError.

Moving to pipeline.py, I'm identifying that `_ordered` needs to sort by received
sequence and line rather than feed order, and that `_admit` uses the raw partner
value for identity instead of the canonical form, which looks like a bug.

Fingerprint sets are correctly keyed by canonical identity. For replay logic, 
the holder lookup uses the wrong identity, and more importantly the current code

record of an identity passing the partner stage should become its holder

batch's line total: K13 defines it as records whose batch field equals that 
batch id, received as part of the line count and fp_xor. Records rejected at 
parse have a null batch and shouldn't count toward any batch, so I need to check
whether the current code correctly filters by item.batch from the raw document 
rather than including rejected records.

set even though the stored fingerprint is null, so for the XOR calculation I

The current code grabs records[line]["fingerprint"] and skips when it's None, 
which is exactly the bug — I should count and XOR any record with a non-null 
batch, since the receipt is meant to confirm that lines of a batch arrived as 
sent, meaning it's computed over all admitted lines regardless of whether the 
fingerprint field itself got set. The mismatches are likely deliberately

itself be "replayed" since it's the first record by definition, but when a new 
record supersedes it, that new record becomes the holder going forward, and 
later records with the same fingerprint get replayed against it.

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Rewrite planner as label-setting search

Thought:
Now the planner:

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/repo/src/migrakit/core/planner.py"
  old_str: 
    def _better(best: tuple[int, list[str]] | None, candidate: tuple[int, 
list[str]]) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
        """Prefer the cheaper plan, then the shorter one; keep the first plan 
found otherwise."""
        if best is None:
            return candidate
        if (candidate[0], len(candidate[1])) < (best[0], len(best[1])):
            return candidate
        return best
    
    
    def _enumerate_paths(
        registry: Registry,
        domain: str,
        origin: FieldView,
        node: str,
        target: str,
        names: list[str],
        state: tuple[str, ...],
        path: list[str],
        visited: set[str],
        cost: int,
        best: list,
    ) -> None:
        if node == target:
            best[0] = _better(best[0], (cost, list(path)))
            return
        for edge in registry.edges_from.get(node, ()):
            if edge.target in visited:
                continue
            if not edge_allowed(registry, domain, origin, edge):
                continue
            visited.add(edge.target)
            path.append(edge.edge_id)
            following = _advance(registry, domain, names, state, edge)
            _enumerate_paths(registry, domain, origin, edge.target, target, 
names, following, path, visited, cost + edge.cost, best)
            path.pop()
            visited.discard(edge.target)
    
    
    def plan_migration(registry: Registry, schema_id: str, payload: dict) -> 
list[str]:
        """Cheapest plan to the canonical schema."""
        domain = registry.domain_of(schema_id)
        target = registry.canonical.get(domain)
        if target is None:
            raise MigrationError("E_UNREACHABLE", f"domain {domain} has no 
canonical schema")
        if schema_id == target:
            return []
        names = registry.named_fields(domain)
        origin = FieldView.from_payload(payload)
        best: list = [None]
        _enumerate_paths(registry, domain, origin, schema_id, target, names, 
origin.states(names), [], {schema_id}, 0, best)
        if best[0] is None:
            raise MigrationError("E_UNREACHABLE", f"no feasible plan from 
{schema_id} to {target}")
        return best[0][1]

  new_str: 
    def _better(best: tuple[int, list[str]] | None, candidate: tuple[int, 
list[str]]) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
        """Prefer the cheaper plan, then the one whose edge id list is smaller 
(M8)."""
        if best is None or candidate < best:
            return candidate
        return best
    
    
    def _enumerate_paths(
        registry: Registry,
        domain: str,
        names: list[str],
        source: str,
        target: str,
        state: tuple[str, ...],
    ) -> list[str] | None:
        """Best plan from ``source`` to ``target``, searching over (schema, 
field state).
    
        A label-setting search: the labels ``(cost, edge ids)`` are popped in M8
order,
        and because every edge cost is positive, a label that loses on one state
can
        never win once extended, so one label per state is enough. Schemas may 
repeat
        on a plan, states may not.
        """
        start = (source, state)
        best: dict[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], tuple[int, list[str]]] = {start:
(0, [])}
        queue: list[tuple[int, list[str], str, tuple[str, ...]]] = [(0, [], 
source, state)]
        while queue:
            cost, path, node, state = heapq.heappop(queue)
            if best[(node, state)] != (cost, path):
                continue
            if node == target:
                return path
            view = FieldView(dict(zip(names, state)))
            for edge in registry.edges_from.get(node, ()):
                if not edge_allowed(registry, domain, view, edge):
                    continue
                following = _advance(registry, domain, names, state, edge)
                label = (cost + edge.cost, path + [edge.edge_id])
                reached = (edge.target, following)
                if _better(best.get(reached), label) is label:
                    best[reached] = label
                    heapq.heappush(queue, (label[0], label[1], edge.target, 
following))
        return None
    
    
    def plan_migration(registry: Registry, schema_id: str, payload: dict) -> 
list[str]:
        """Cheapest plan to the canonical schema."""
        domain = registry.domain_of(schema_id)
        target = registry.canonical.get(domain)
        if target is None:
            raise MigrationError("E_UNREACHABLE", f"domain {domain} has no 
canonical schema")
        if schema_id == target:
            return []
        names = registry.named_fields(domain)
        state = FieldView.from_payload(payload).states(names)
        plan = _enumerate_paths(registry, domain, names, schema_id, target, 
state)
        if plan is None:
            raise MigrationError("E_UNREACHABLE", f"no feasible plan from 
{schema_id} to {target}")
        return plan

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Clean up search loop

Reasoning:
I need to import heapq, and I'm reconsidering the comparison logic for updating 
the best label—rewriting it as a plain None-or-less-than check is clearer than 
relying on an identity trick, though that leaves the helper function unused, 
which I want to avoid.

I'll settle on checking whether the better of the two values equals the new 
label, which reads naturally as "this label wins," and it's safe since duplicate
paths producing identical labels shouldn't occur given how paths are generated 
deterministically. I also want to rename the shadowed loop variable for clarity 
before cleaning up the function body.



Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/repo/src/migrakit/core/planner.py"
  old_str: 
        start = (source, state)
        best: dict[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], tuple[int, list[str]]] = {start:
(0, [])}
        queue: list[tuple[int, list[str], str, tuple[str, ...]]] = [(0, [], 
source, state)]
        while queue:
            cost, path, node, state = heapq.heappop(queue)
            if best[(node, state)] != (cost, path):
                continue
            if node == target:
                return path
            view = FieldView(dict(zip(names, state)))
            for edge in registry.edges_from.get(node, ()):
                if not edge_allowed(registry, domain, view, edge):
                    continue
                following = _advance(registry, domain, names, state, edge)
                label = (cost + edge.cost, path + [edge.edge_id])
                reached = (edge.target, following)
                if _better(best.get(reached), label) is label:
                    best[reached] = label
                    heapq.heappush(queue, (label[0], label[1], edge.target, 
following))
        return None

  new_str: 
        best: dict[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], tuple[int, list[str]]] = 
{(source, state): (0, [])}
        queue: list[tuple[int, list[str], str, tuple[str, ...]]] = [(0, [], 
source, state)]
        while queue:
            cost, path, node, fields = heapq.heappop(queue)
            if best[(node, fields)] != (cost, path):
                continue
            if node == target:
                return path
            view = FieldView(dict(zip(names, fields)))
            for edge in registry.edges_from.get(node, ()):
                if not edge_allowed(registry, domain, view, edge):
                    continue
                reached = (edge.target, _advance(registry, domain, names, 
fields, edge))
                label = (cost + edge.cost, path + [edge.edge_id])
                if _better(best.get(reached), label) == label:
                    best[reached] = label
                    heapq.heappush(queue, (label[0], label[1], *reached))
        return None

Thought:
Now the executor:

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/repo/src/migrakit/core/executor.py"
  old_str: 
    def execute_plan(
        registry: Registry, schema_id: str, payload: dict, plan: list[str], 
corrections: list[dict]
    ) -> dict:
        """Execute ``plan`` from ``schema_id`` after applying the record's 
corrections."""
        domain = registry.domain_of(schema_id)
        record = copy.deepcopy(payload)
        applied: list[str] = []
        for correction in corrections:
            _apply_correction(record, correction)
            applied.append(correction["id"])
        current = schema_id
        for step, edge_id in enumerate(plan):
            edge = registry.edges.get(edge_id)
            if edge is None or edge.source != current:
                raise ValueError(f"plan step {step} ({edge_id}) does not leave 
{current}")
            record = apply_operations(registry, domain, record, edge.operations)
            current = edge.target
        return {
            "status": "migrated",
            "code": None,
            "schema": current,
            "payload": record,
            "completed_steps": len(plan),
            "failed_edge": None,
            "corrections_applied": applied,
        }

  new_str: 
    def _arrive(record: dict, schema: str, pinned: dict[str, list[dict]], 
applied: list[str]) -> None:
        """Apply the corrections pinned to ``schema``, once, on first arrival 
there (C4)."""
        for correction in pinned.pop(schema, ()):
            _apply_correction(record, correction)
            applied.append(correction["id"])
    
    
    def execute_plan(
        registry: Registry, schema_id: str, payload: dict, plan: list[str], 
corrections: list[dict]
    ) -> dict:
        """Execute ``plan`` from ``schema_id``, applying the record's 
corrections on the way."""
        domain = registry.domain_of(schema_id)
        record = copy.deepcopy(payload)
        pinned: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
        for correction in corrections:
            pinned.setdefault(correction["schema"], []).append(correction)
        applied: list[str] = []
        current = schema_id
        _arrive(record, current, pinned, applied)
        for step, edge_id in enumerate(plan):
            edge = registry.edges.get(edge_id)
            if edge is None or edge.source != current:
                raise ValueError(f"plan step {step} ({edge_id}) does not leave 
{current}")
            if not edge_allowed(registry, domain, 
FieldView.from_payload(record), edge):
                return {
                    "status": "failed",
                    "code": "E_PREREQ_FAILED",
                    "schema": current,
                    "payload": None,
                    "completed_steps": step,
                    "failed_edge": edge_id,
                    "corrections_applied": applied,
                }
            record = apply_operations(registry, domain, record, edge.operations)
            current = edge.target
            _arrive(record, current, pinned, applied)
        return {
            "status": "migrated",
            "code": None,
            "schema": current,
            "payload": record,
            "completed_steps": len(plan),
            "failed_edge": None,
            "corrections_applied": applied,
        }

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

  command: "str_replace"

  old_str: 
        if not isinstance(value, str):
            raise MigrationError("E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER", repr(value))
        if value in registry.partners:
            partner_id = value
        elif value in registry.aliases:
            partner_id = registry.aliases[value]
        else:
            raise MigrationError("E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER", value)
        return registry.merges.get(partner_id, partner_id)

  new_str: 
        if not isinstance(value, str):
            raise MigrationError("E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER", repr(value))
        if value in registry.partners:
            partner_id = value
        elif value in registry.aliases:
            partner_id = registry.aliases[value]
        else:
            raise MigrationError("E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER", value)
        seen = {partner_id}
        while partner_id in registry.merges:
            partner_id = registry.merges[partner_id]
            if partner_id in seen:
                raise MigrationError("E_ALIAS_CYCLE", value)
            seen.add(partner_id)
        return partner_id

Summary: Fix tokens, receipts and processing order

Reasoning:
I'm now updating pipeline.py: ordering lines by received batch and line number, 
matching tokens by exact equality, admitting canonical identities into the 
holder set, and computing receipt mismatches by XOR-ing record fingerprints per 
batch, including handling the fingerprint for unknown parts.

Writing receipt mismatch logic...

Including parse-rejected lines wouldn't make sense since fingerprints are 
undefined there, confirming my interpretation is correct. Now I'm writing the 
admission logic: resolving the partner, computing the fingerprint, tracking it 
by identity, and checking against any existing holder to determine status based 
on whether fingerprints match...

I need to confirm that a replayed record's fingerprint still gets added to the 
identity's set for future REISSUE matching—yes, since REISSUE should match any 
record with that partner and key, not just the latest one. Now I'm considering 
whether the holder should update even when the migration fails or gets rejected.

For the replay/conflict check, the comparison should be against the current 
holder's fingerprint specifically, not any historical record—so if fingerprint A
is superseded by B, and then A reappears, it conflicts with B unless a REISSUE 
explicitly matches A. That matches my implementation. I'm now wondering about 
edge cases where a "superseded" record somehow also carries a "replayed" status.

A holder itself can't be marked replayed. If a record gets rejected with 
E_REPLAY_CONFLICT, it shouldn't become the holder but its fingerprint still 
needs to go into the tracked set. I also want to verify `_migrate` correctly 
maps tokens to the canonical partner via the identity tuple. For ordering, I'm 
sorting admitted lines by their batch's received sequence and then by line 
number, confirming batch lookups are only done for admitted lines. The 
pipeline's parse check also looks fine. Time to write it up.



Action: FileEditorAction

  old_str: 
    def _replace_tokens(payload: dict, tokens: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict:
        """Replace the partner's null tokens with explicit nulls; values are 
compared trimmed, as the tokens are."""
        return {name: (None if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() in 
tokens else value) for name, value in payload.items()}
    
    
    def _receipt_mismatches(items: list[FeedLine], records: dict[int, dict], 
receipts: dict[str, tuple[int, str]]) -> list[str]:
        """Batches whose lines or fingerprint XOR differ from the manifest 
receipt."""
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        xors: dict[str, int] = {}
        for item in items:
            batch = item.batch
            if batch not in receipts:
                continue
            counts[batch] = counts.get(batch, 0) + 1
            fingerprint = records[item.line]["fingerprint"]
            if fingerprint is not None:
                xors[batch] = xors.get(batch, 0) ^ int(fingerprint[:8], 16)
        return sorted(
            batch
            for batch, (lines, fp_xor) in receipts.items()
            if counts.get(batch, 0) != lines or f"{xors.get(batch, 0):08x}" != 
fp_xor
        )
    
    
    def _ordered(lines: list[FeedLine], received: dict[str, int]) -> 
list[FeedLine]:
        """Processing order of the admitted lines."""
        return list(lines)

  new_str: 
    def _replace_tokens(payload: dict, tokens: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict:
        """Replace the partner's null tokens with explicit nulls; a value must 
equal a token exactly."""

value) for name, value in payload.items()}
    
    
    def _receipt_mismatches(items: list[FeedLine], records: dict[int, dict], 
receipts: dict[str, tuple[int, str]]) -> list[str]:
        """Batches whose lines or fingerprint XOR differ from the manifest 
receipt."""
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        xors: dict[str, int] = {}
        for item in items:
            batch = records[item.line]["batch"]
            if batch not in receipts:
                continue
            counts[batch] = counts.get(batch, 0) + 1
            fingerprint = record_fingerprint(item.record["schema"], 
item.record["payload"])
            xors[batch] = xors.get(batch, 0) ^ int(fingerprint[:8], 16)
        return sorted(
            batch
            for batch, (lines, fp_xor) in receipts.items()
            if counts.get(batch, 0) != lines or f"{xors.get(batch, 0):08x}" != 
fp_xor

by line."""

import csv,json,collections
sv=json.load(open('schema_versions.json'))['schemas']
dom={s['id']:s['domain'] for s in sv}
can={s['domain']:s['id'] for s in sv if s.get('canonical')}
edges=list(csv.DictReader(open('migration_edges.csv')))
adj=collections.defaultdict(list)
for e in edges: adj[e['source']].append(e['target'])
# reachability ignoring prereqs
unreach=[]
for s in sv:
    t=can[s['domain']]
    seen={s['id']};st=[s['id']]
    while st:
        n=st.pop()
        for m in adj[n]:
            if m not in seen: seen.add(m); st.append(m)
    if t not in seen: unreach.append(s['id'])
print('schemas that cannot reach canonical ignoring prereqs:', len(unreach))
feed=[json.loads(l) for l in open('partner_batches.jsonl')]
c=collections.Counter(r['schema'] for r in feed if isinstance(r,dict) and 
isinstance(r.get('schema'),str))
print('feed lines on those schemas:', sum(c[s] for s in unreach))
print('feed lines with schema not in sv:', sum(v for k,v in c.items() if k not 
in dom))
"

$ cd /app/repo && PYTHONPATH=src python -c "
import json, collections, migrakit
from migrakit import load_contracts
from migrakit.parser.corrections import parse_corrections
reg=load_contracts('/app/ci')
cor,mal=parse_corrections(open('/app/ci/correction_log.txt').read())
recs, summary = 
migrakit.run_pipeline('/app/ci','/app/ci/partner_batches.jsonl','/app/ci/correct
ion_log.txt','/app/ci/replay_manifest.csv')
inv=set(summary['invalid_corrections'])
valid=[c for c in cor if c.op!='REISSUE' and c.correction_id not in inv]
bykey={}
for r in recs:
    if r['plan']: bykey.setdefault((r['partner'],r['key']),[]).append(r)
hits=0; samples=[]
for c in valid:
    pid=migrakit.resolve_partner(reg,c.partner)
    for r in bykey.get((pid,c.key),[]):
        plan=r['plan']
        sources=[reg.edges[e].source for e in plan]
        if c.schema not in sources: continue
        i=sources.index(c.schema)
        later=set()
        for e in plan[i:]:
            for k,n in reg.edges[e].requires: later.add(n)
        if c.field in later:
            hits+=1
            if len(samples)<5: 
samples.append((c.correction_id,c.op,c.field,c.schema,plan,r['line'],r['status']
))
print('corrections whose field is required later:',hits)
for s in samples: print(s)
"

import json, collections, migrakit
from migrakit import load_contracts
from migrakit.parser.corrections import parse_corrections
reg=load_contracts('/app/ci')
cor,mal=parse_corrections(open('/app/ci/correction_log.txt').read())
steps=[c for c in cor if c.op!='REISSUE']
inprereq=0; innamed=0; unknownschema=0
for c in steps:
    d=reg.schemas.get(c.schema)
    if d is None: unknownschema+=1; continue
    if c.field in reg.prerequisite_fields(d): inprereq+=1
    if c.field in reg.named_fields(d): innamed+=1
print(len(steps), 'unknown schema', unknownschema, 'field is prereq field', 
inprereq, 'field named', innamed)
print('avg prereq fields', sum(len(reg.prerequisite_fields(d)) for d in 
set(reg.schemas.values()))/100.0)
print('avg named', sum(len(reg.named_fields(d)) for d in 
set(reg.schemas.values()))/100.0)

import json, csv, collections
from migrakit import record_fingerprint
rows=list(csv.DictReader(open('/app/ci/replay_manifest.csv')))
receipts={r['batch_id']:(int(r['lines']),r['fp_xor']) for r in rows}
lines=open('/app/ci/partner_batches.jsonl').read().splitlines()
docs=[]
for l in lines:
    try: docs.append(json.loads(l))
    except Exception: docs.append(None)
def report(include_bad):
    c=collections.Counter(); x=collections.defaultdict(int)
    for d in docs:
        if not isinstance(d,dict): continue
        b=d.get('batch')
        if not isinstance(b,str) or b not in receipts: continue
        ok=all(isinstance(d.get(k),str) and d[k] for k in 
('batch','partner','key','schema')) and isinstance(d.get('payload'),dict)
        if not ok and not include_bad: continue
        c[b]+=1
        if isinstance(d.get('payload'),dict) and 
isinstance(d.get('schema'),str):
            x[b]^=int(record_fingerprint(d['schema'],d['payload'])[:8],16)
    mm=sorted(b for b,(n,fx) in receipts.items() if c[b]!=n or '%08x'%x[b]!=fx)
    return mm
print('exclude bad:',report(False))
print('include bad:',report(True))

$ cd /app/repo && PYTHONPATH=src timeout 1200 python -c "
import json, random, migrakit
from migrakit import load_contracts, plan_migration
from migrakit.core.records import FieldView, edge_allowed
from migrakit.core.operations import apply_operations
from migrakit.core.planner import _stand_in
reg=load_contracts('/app/ci')

def brute(schema, payload, maxdepth=7):
    dom=reg.schemas[schema]; target=reg.canonical[dom]
    names=reg.named_fields(dom)
    start=FieldView.from_payload(payload).states(names)
    best=[None]
    def dfs(node, state, path, cost, depth):
        if best[0] is not None and (cost,path) > best[0] and cost>=best[0][0]:
            pass
        if node==target:
            cand=(cost,list(path))
            if best[0] is None or cand<best[0]: best[0]=cand
            return
        if depth==0: return
        view=FieldView(dict(zip(names,state)))
        for e in reg.edges_from.get(node,()):
            if not edge_allowed(reg,dom,view,e): continue
            rec=apply_operations(reg,dom,_stand_in(names,state),e.operations)
            ns=FieldView.from_payload(rec).states(names)
            path.append(e.edge_id)
            dfs(e.target, ns, path, cost+e.cost, depth-1)
            path.pop()
    dfs(schema,start,[],0,maxdepth)
    return best[0]

feed=[json.loads(l) for l in open('/app/ci/partner_batches.jsonl') if 
l.strip().startswith('{')]
random.seed(1)
sample=random.sample(feed, 120)
mismatch=0; checked=0
for d in sample:
    s=d.get('schema'); p=d.get('payload')
    if not isinstance(s,str) or s not in reg.schemas or not isinstance(p,dict): 
continue
    tok=reg.tokens_for('X')
    payload={k:(None if isinstance(v,str) and v in tok else v) for k,v in 
p.items()}
    try:
        got=plan_migration(reg,s,payload)
    except migrakit.MigrationError:
        got=None
    b=brute(s,payload)
    exp = b[1] if b else None
    checked+=1
    if got!=exp:
        mismatch+=1
        print('MISMATCH',s,got,exp)
print('checked',checked,'mismatch',mismatch)
" [timeout: 1200.0s]

import json, random, migrakit
from migrakit import load_contracts, plan_migration
from migrakit.core.records import FieldView, edge_allowed
from migrakit.core.operations import apply_operations
from migrakit.core.planner import _stand_in
reg=load_contracts('/app/ci')
def brute(schema, payload, maxdepth=7):
    dom=reg.schemas[schema]; target=reg.canonical[dom]
    names=reg.named_fields(dom)
    start=FieldView.from_payload(payload).states(names)
    best=[None]
    def dfs(node, state, path, cost, depth):
        if best[0] is not None and (cost,path) > best[0] and cost>=best[0][0]:
            pass
        if node==target:
            cand=(cost,list(path))
            if best[0] is None or cand<best[0]: best[0]=cand
            return
        if depth==0: return
        view=FieldView(dict(zip(names,state)))
        for e in reg.edges_from.get(node,()):
            if not edge_allowed(reg,dom,view,e): continue
            rec=apply_operations(reg,dom,_stand_in(names,state),e.operations)
            ns=FieldView.from_payload(rec).states(names)
            path.append(e.edge_id)
            dfs(e.target, ns, path, cost+e.cost, depth-1)
            path.pop()
    dfs(schema,start,[],0,maxdepth)
    return best[0]
feed=[json.loads(l) for l in open('/app/ci/partner_batches.jsonl') if 
l.strip().startswith('{')]
random.seed(1)
sample=random.sample(feed, 120)
mismatch=0; checked=0
for d in sample:
    s=d.get('schema'); p=d.get('payload')
    if not isinstance(s,str) or s not in reg.schemas or not isinstance(p,dict): 
continue
    tok=reg.tokens_for('X')
    payload={k:(None if isinstance(v,str) and v in tok else v) for k,v in 
p.items()}
    try:
        got=plan_migration(reg,s,payload)
    except migrakit.MigrationError:
        got=None
    b=brute(s,payload)
    exp = b[1] if b else None
    checked+=1
    if got!=exp:
        mismatch+=1
        print('MISMATCH',s,got,exp)
print('checked',checked,'mismatch',mismatch)

class _Run:
    def __init__(self, registry: Registry, corrections: list[Correction]) -> 
None:
        self.registry = registry
        self.invalid: set[str] = set()
        self.steps: dict[tuple[str, str], list[Correction]] = {}
        self.reissues: dict[tuple[str, str], list[Correction]] = {}
        for correction in corrections:
            try:
                partner_id = resolve_partner(registry, correction.partner)
            except MigrationError:
                self.invalid.add(correction.correction_id)
                continue
            target = self.reissues if correction.op == "REISSUE" else self.steps
            target.setdefault((partner_id, correction.key), 
[]).append(correction)
        self.holders: dict[tuple[str, str], dict] = {}
        self.fingerprints: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]] = {}
        self.used: set[str] = set()

    def _admit(self, item: FeedLine) -> dict:
        document = item.record
        record = _blank_record(item.line, document)
        try:
            partner_id = resolve_partner(self.registry, document["partner"])
        except MigrationError as exc:
            record["code"] = exc.code
            return record
        record["partner"] = partner_id
        fingerprint = record_fingerprint(document["schema"], 
document["payload"])
        record["fingerprint"] = fingerprint
        identity = (partner_id, document["key"])
        self.fingerprints.setdefault(identity, set()).add(fingerprint)
        holder = self.holders.get(identity)
        if holder is not None:
            if holder["fingerprint"] == fingerprint:
                record["status"] = "replayed"
                return record
            if not any(fingerprint.startswith(r.fingerprint_prefix) for r in 
self.reissues.get(identity, ())):
                record["code"] = "E_REPLAY_CONFLICT"
                return record
            holder.update(status="superseded", code=None, payload=None)
        self._migrate(record, document, identity)
        self.holders[identity] = record
        return record

    def _migrate(self, record: dict, document: dict, identity: tuple[str, str]) 
-> None:
        payload = _replace_tokens(document["payload"], 
self.registry.tokens_for(identity[0]))
        try:
            plan = plan_migration(self.registry, document["schema"], payload)
        except MigrationError as exc:
            record["code"] = exc.code
            return
        steps = [correction.as_step() for correction in self.steps.get(identity,
())]
        result = execute_plan(self.registry, document["schema"], payload, plan, 
steps)
        self.used.update(result["corrections_applied"])
        status, code = classify_result(plan, result)
        record.update(
            status=status,
            code=code,
            schema=result["schema"],
            plan=plan,

    """Contract checks on small synthetic contract folders (not part of the 
repository)."""
    
    import json
    import os
    import subprocess
    import sys
    import tempfile
    import unittest
    from pathlib import Path
    
    ROOT = Path("/app/repo")
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
    
    import migrakit  # noqa: E402
    
    TAXONOMY = {
        "categories": [
            {"name": "complete", "members": ["migrated"]},
            {"name": "duplicate", "members": ["replayed", "superseded"]},
            {"name": "execution_failure", "members": ["E_PREREQ_FAILED"]},
            {"name": "conflict", "members": ["E_REPLAY_CONFLICT"]},
            {"name": "input_error", "members": ["E_PARSE", "E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER", 
"E_ALIAS_CYCLE"]},
            {"name": "planning_error", "members": ["E_UNKNOWN_SCHEMA", 
"E_UNREACHABLE"]},
            {"name": "other", "members": ["failed", "rejected"]},
        ]
    }
    
    
    def write(folder, schemas, edges, policy, ini, aliases, 
order="partner_id\trank\nP1\t1\n"):
        folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (folder / "schema_versions.json").write_text(json.dumps({"schemas": 
schemas}), encoding="utf-8")
        (folder / "migration_edges.csv").write_text("\n".join(edges) + "\n", 
encoding="utf-8")
        (folder / "field_policy.tsv").write_text("\n".join(policy) + "\n", 
encoding="utf-8")
        (folder / "null_semantics.ini").write_text(ini, encoding="utf-8")
        (folder / "partner_aliases.xml").write_text(aliases, encoding="utf-8")
        (folder / "report_taxonomy.json").write_text(json.dumps(TAXONOMY), 
encoding="utf-8")
        (folder / "partner_order.tsv").write_text(order, encoding="utf-8")
        return folder
    
    
    SCHEMAS = [
        {"id": "d.v1", "domain": "d", "canonical": False},
        {"id": "d.v2", "domain": "d", "canonical": False},
        {"id": "d.v3", "domain": "d", "canonical": True},
    ]
    POLICY = [
        "domain\tfield\tdefault\tfill\tnull_counts_as",
        "d\ta\t1\tabsent_or_null\t",
        "d\tb\t\t\tabsent",
        "d\tc\t\"x\"\t\tpresent",
        "d\tz\t2\t\t",
    ]
    INI = (
        "[presence]\nexplicit_null = present\n\n[fill]\nmode = absent_only\n\n"
        "[tokens]\ndefault = NULL | N/A\nP2 = -\nP3 =\n"
    )
    ALIASES = (
        '<partners>\n'
        '  <partner id="P1"><alias>one</alias></partner>\n'
        '  <partner id="P2"><alias>two</alias></partner>\n'
        '  <partner id="P3"><alias>three</alias></partner>\n'
        '  <partner id="P4"/><partner id="P5"/><partner id="P6"/><partner 
id="P7"/>\n'
        '  <merge from="P4" into="P5"/><merge from="P5" into="P1"/>\n'
        '  <merge from="P6" into="P7"/><merge from="P7" into="P6"/>\n'
        '</partners>\n'
    )
    
    
    class Checks(unittest.TestCase):
        def setUp(self):
            self.scratch = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="migrakit-checks-"))
    
        def contracts(self, edges, schemas=SCHEMAS, policy=POLICY, ini=INI, 
aliases=ALIASES, order="partner_id\trank\nP2\t5\nP1\t9\n"):
            folder = write(self.scratch / 
f"c{len(list(self.scratch.iterdir()))}", schemas, edges, policy, ini, aliases, 
order)
            return migrakit.load_contracts(folder)
    
        # ---- partner identity (P rules) -------------------------------------
        def test_merge_chain(self):
            reg = 
self.contracts(["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost"])
            self.assertEqual(migrakit.resolve_partner(reg, "P4"), "P1")
            self.assertEqual(migrakit.resolve_partner(reg, "one"), "P1")
    
        def test_alias_cycle(self):
            reg = 
self.contracts(["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost"])
            with self.assertRaises(migrakit.MigrationError) as caught:
                migrakit.resolve_partner(reg, "P6")
            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "E_ALIAS_CYCLE")
    
        def test_unknown_partner(self):
            reg = 
self.contracts(["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost"])
            with self.assertRaises(migrakit.MigrationError) as caught:
                migrakit.resolve_partner(reg, "nobody")
            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER")
    
        # ---- state signature (M10, N1) --------------------------------------
        def test_signature_keeps_null(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v2,has:a;lacks:b,,1",
                "A2,d.v2,d.v3,,,1",
            ])
            payload = {"a": None, "q": 1}
            self.assertEqual(migrakit.state_signature(reg, "d.v1", payload), 
{"a": "null", "b": "absent"})
            self.assertEqual(payload, {"a": None, "q": 1})
    
        def test_signature_unknown_schema(self):
            reg = 
self.contracts(["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost"])
            with self.assertRaises(migrakit.MigrationError) as caught:
                migrakit.state_signature(reg, "d.v9", {})
            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "E_UNKNOWN_SCHEMA")
    
        # ---- planning (M6 to M9) --------------------------------------------
        def test_plan_revisits_a_schema(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v2,,drop:b,1",
                "A2,d.v2,d.v1,,,1",
                "A3,d.v1,d.v3,lacks:b,,1",
            ])
            self.assertEqual(migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {"b": 1}), 
["A1", "A2", "A3"])
    
        def test_plan_prefers_cheapest_then_smallest_list(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "B1,d.v1,d.v3,,,5",
                "A9,d.v1,d.v2,,,2",
                "A8,d.v2,d.v3,,,3",
                "A7,d.v1,d.v2,,,2",
            ])
            self.assertEqual(migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {}), ["A7", 
"A8"])
    
        def test_plan_unreachable(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v3,has:b,,1",
            ])
            with self.assertRaises(migrakit.MigrationError) as caught:
                migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {})
            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "E_UNREACHABLE")
    
        def test_plan_sees_payload_as_given(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v3,has:b,,1",
            ])
            # b is an explicit null and policy says it counts as absent for 
domain d
            with self.assertRaises(migrakit.MigrationError):
                migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {"b": None})
            # c counts as present when null
            reg2 = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v3,has:c,,1",
            ])
            self.assertEqual(migrakit.plan_migration(reg2, "d.v1", {"c": None}),
["A1"])
    
        def test_presence_falls_back_to_ini(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v3,has:z,,1",
            ])
            self.assertEqual(migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {"z": None}), 
["A1"])
    
        # ---- operations (M4, M5, N4, N5) ------------------------------------
        def test_fill_modes_and_rename_of_null(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v2,,add:a;add:z,1",
                "A2,d.v2,d.v3,,rename:a>b,1",
            ])
            out = migrakit.execute_plan(reg, "d.v1", {"a": None, "z": None}, 
["A1", "A2"], [])
            # a: fill absent_or_null -> default 1, then renamed to b; z: 
absent_only -> stays null
            self.assertEqual(out["payload"], {"b": 1, "z": None})
    
        def test_casts(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v3,,cast:p:int;cast:q:float;cast:r:str;cast:s:int,1",
            ])
            out = migrakit.execute_plan(reg, "d.v1", {"p": " -12 ", "q": 3, "r":
4.5, "s": True}, ["A1"], [])
            self.assertEqual(out["payload"], {"p": -12, "q": 3.0, "r": "4.5", 
"s": True})
    
        # ---- execution (M11, M12, C rules) ----------------------------------
        def test_corrections_pinned_and_prereq_failure(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v2,,,1",
                "A2,d.v2,d.v3,has:b,,1",
            ])
            plan = migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {"b": 1})
            self.assertEqual(plan, ["A1", "A2"])
            payload = {"b": 1}
            out = migrakit.execute_plan(
                reg, "d.v1", payload, plan,
                [{"id": "C1", "op": "UNSET", "schema": "d.v2", "field": "b", 
"value": None}],
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                (out["status"], out["code"], out["schema"], 
out["completed_steps"], out["failed_edge"], out["payload"]),
                ("failed", "E_PREREQ_FAILED", "d.v2", 1, "A2", None),
            )
            self.assertEqual(out["corrections_applied"], ["C1"])
            self.assertEqual(payload, {"b": 1})
    
        def test_corrections_apply_on_first_arrival_only(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v2,,,1",
                "A2,d.v2,d.v1,,,1",
                "A3,d.v1,d.v3,has:m,,1",
            ])
            corrections = [
                {"id": "C1", "op": "SET", "schema": "d.v1", "field": "m", 
"value": 7},
                {"id": "C2", "op": "SET", "schema": "d.v2", "field": "n", 
"value": 8},
                {"id": "C3", "op": "SET", "schema": "d.v1", "field": "m", 
"value": 9},
            ]
            out = migrakit.execute_plan(reg, "d.v1", {}, ["A1", "A2", "A3"], 
corrections)
            self.assertEqual(out["status"], "migrated")
            self.assertEqual(out["payload"], {"m": 9, "n": 8})
            self.assertEqual(out["corrections_applied"], ["C1", "C3", "C2"])
    
        def test_correction_at_the_final_schema(self):
            reg = self.contracts([
                "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
                "A1,d.v1,d.v3,,,1",
            ])
            out = migrakit.execute_plan(
                reg, "d.v1", {}, ["A1"],
                [{"id": "C1", "op": "NULL", "schema": "d.v3", "field": "k", 
"value": None}],
            )
            self.assertEqual(out["payload"], {"k": None})
            self.assertEqual(out["corrections_applied"], ["C1"])
    
        # ---- pipeline (K13 to K18) ------------------------------------------
        def run_feed(self, edges, feed_lines, corrections, manifest, **kwargs):
            folder = write(
                self.scratch / f"c{len(list(self.scratch.iterdir()))}", SCHEMAS,
edges, POLICY, INI, ALIASES,
                kwargs.get("order", "partner_id\trank\nP2\t5\nP1\t9\n"),
            )
            feed = folder / "feed.jsonl"
            feed.write_text("".join(json.dumps(line) + "\n" if isinstance(line, 
dict) else line + "\n" for line in feed_lines), encoding="utf-8")
            (folder / "corr.txt").write_text(corrections, encoding="utf-8")
            (folder / "man.csv").write_text(manifest, encoding="utf-8")
            return migrakit.run_pipeline(folder, feed, folder / "corr.txt", 
folder / "man.csv")
    
        def test_processing_order_and_replay(self):
            edges = ["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost", 
"A1,d.v1,d.v3,,,1"]
            # line 2 belongs to the batch received first, so it holds the 
identity
            feed = [
                {"batch": "B2", "partner": "one", "key": "k", "schema": "d.v1", 
"payload": {"v": 1}},
                {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P1", "key": "k", "schema": "d.v1", 
"payload": {"v": 2}},
            ]
            fp1 = migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": 1})
            fp2 = migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": 2})
            manifest = (
                "batch_id,received_seq,lines,fp_xor\n"
                f"B1,1,1,{fp2[:8]}\nB2,2,1,{fp1[:8]}\n"
            )
            records, summary = self.run_feed(edges, feed, "# none\n", manifest)
            self.assertEqual([r["status"] for r in records], ["rejected", 
"migrated"])
            self.assertEqual(records[0]["code"], "E_REPLAY_CONFLICT")
            self.assertEqual(summary["receipt_mismatches"], [])
            # the reissue of the later-processed line supersedes the holder
            records, summary = self.run_feed(edges, feed, f"X1 REISSUE P1 k 
{fp1[:12]}\n", manifest)
            self.assertEqual([r["status"] for r in records], ["migrated", 
"superseded"])
            self.assertEqual(records[1]["payload"], None)
            self.assertEqual(records[1]["code"], None)
            self.assertEqual(summary["by_category"]["duplicate"], 1)
            self.assertEqual(summary["invalid_corrections"], [])
    
        def test_parse_and_receipts_and_partner_order(self):
            edges = ["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost", 
"A1,d.v1,d.v3,,,1"]
            feed = [
                "{not json",
                {"batch": "B1", "partner": "two", "key": "k", "schema": "d.v1", 
"payload": {"v": "-"}},
                {"batch": "B9", "partner": "P1", "key": "k", "schema": "d.v1", 
"payload": {}},
                {"batch": "B1", "partner": "nobody", "key": "k", "schema": 
"d.v1", "payload": {}},
                {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P1", "key": "k2", "schema": "d.v8", 
"payload": {}},
            ]
            manifest = "batch_id,received_seq,lines,fp_xor\nB1,1,9,00000000\n"
            records, summary = self.run_feed(edges, feed, "# none\n", manifest)
            self.assertEqual([r["status"] for r in records], ["rejected"] * 5)
            self.assertEqual([r["code"] for r in records], ["E_PARSE", None, 
"E_PARSE", "E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER", "E_UNKNOWN_SCHEMA"])
            self.assertEqual(records[0]["batch"], None)
            self.assertEqual(records[2]["batch"], None)
            self.assertEqual(records[3]["fingerprint"], None)
            self.assertEqual(summary["receipt_mismatches"], ["B1"])
            self.assertEqual([p["partner"] for p in summary["partners"]], ["P2",
"P1"])
            self.assertEqual(summary["by_category"], {
                "complete": 0, "duplicate": 0, "execution_failure": 0, 
"conflict": 0,
                "input_error": 3, "planning_error": 1, "other": 0,
            })
    
        def test_tokens_per_partner(self):
            edges = ["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost", 
"A1,d.v1,d.v3,lacks:v,,1"]
            feed = [
                {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P2", "key": "k1", "schema": "d.v1", 
"payload": {"v": "-"}},
                {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P1", "key": "k2", "schema": "d.v1", 
"payload": {"v": "-"}},
                {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P3", "key": "k3", "schema": "d.v1", 
"payload": {"v": "NULL"}},
                {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P1", "key": "k4", "schema": "d.v1", 
"payload": {"v": " NULL "}},
            ]
            manifest = "batch_id,received_seq,lines,fp_xor\nB1,1,4,%s\n" % 
"%08x" % (
                int(migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": "-"})[:8], 16)
                ^ int(migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": "-"})[:8], 16)
                ^ int(migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": "NULL"})[:8], 
16)
                ^ int(migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": " NULL "})[:8], 
16)
            )
            records, summary = self.run_feed(edges, feed, "# none\n", manifest)
            # P2's own token line replaces the default, P3's line is empty, and 
tokens match exactly
            self.assertEqual([r["status"] for r in records], ["rejected", 
"migrated", "rejected", "migrated"])
            self.assertEqual(records[1]["payload"], {"v": None})
            self.assertEqual(summary["receipt_mismatches"], [])
    
        def test_invalid_corrections(self):
            edges = ["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost", 
"A1,d.v1,d.v3,,,1"]
            feed = [{"batch": "B1", "partner": "P1", "key": "k", "schema": 
"d.v1", "payload": {}}]
            manifest = "batch_id,received_seq,lines,fp_xor\nB1,1,1,%s\n" % 
migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {})[:8]
            log = (
                "# comment\n\n"
                "X1 SET P1 k AT d.v1 w 5\n"
                "X2 SET nobody k AT d.v1 w 5\n"
                "X3 SET P1 other AT d.v1 w 5\n"
                "X4 SET P1 k AT d.v2 w 5\n"
                "X5 BOGUS P1 k\n"
                "X6 REISSUE P1 k 0123456789ab\n"
            )
            records, summary = self.run_feed(edges, feed, log, manifest)
            self.assertEqual(records[0]["payload"], {"w": 5})
            self.assertEqual(summary["invalid_corrections"], ["X2", "X3", "X4", 
"X5", "X6"])
            self.assertEqual(summary["records"], 1)
            self.assertEqual(summary["attempted"], 1)
    
        def test_cli_writes_outputs(self):
            folder = write(self.scratch / "cli", SCHEMAS, 
["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost", "A1,d.v1,d.v3,,,1"], POLICY, 
INI, ALIASES)
            (folder / "feed.jsonl").write_text(json.dumps({"batch": "B1", 
"partner": "P1", "key": "k", "schema": "d.v1", "payload": {}}) + "\n", 
encoding="utf-8")
            (folder / "corr.txt").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
            (folder / 
"man.csv").write_text("batch_id,received_seq,lines,fp_xor\nB1,1,1,%s\n" % 
migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {})[:8], encoding="utf-8")
            env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(ROOT / "src"))
            done = subprocess.run(
                [sys.executable, "-m", "migrakit", "run", "--contracts", ".", 
"--feed", "feed.jsonl",
                 "--corrections", "corr.txt", "--manifest", "man.csv", "--out", 
"out/deep"],
                capture_output=True, text=True, env=env, cwd=folder,
            )
            self.assertEqual(done.returncode, 0, done.stderr)
            record = json.loads((folder / "out" / "deep" / 
"records.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            summary = json.loads((folder / "out" / "deep" / 
"run_summary.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.assertEqual(record["status"], "migrated")
            self.assertEqual(summary["by_status"], {"migrated": 1})
            self.assertEqual(summary["receipt_mismatches"], [])
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        unittest.main(verbosity=2)

+9|from pathlib import Path

+11|ROOT = Path("/app/repo")
+12|sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
+13|
+14|import migrakit  # noqa: E402
+15|
+16|TAXONOMY = {
+17|    "categories": [
+18|        {"name": "complete", "members": ["migrated"]},
+19|        {"name": "duplicate", "members": ["replayed", "superseded"]},
+20|        {"name": "execution_failure", "members": ["E_PREREQ_FAILED"]},
+21|        {"name": "conflict", "members": ["E_REPLAY_CONFLICT"]},
+22|        {"name": "input_error", "members": ["E_PARSE", "E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER", 
"E_ALIAS_CYCLE"]},
+23|        {"name": "planning_error", "members": ["E_UNKNOWN_SCHEMA", 
"E_UNREACHABLE"]},
+24|        {"name": "other", "members": ["failed", "rejected"]},
+25|    ]
+26|}
+27|
+28|
+29|def write(folder, schemas, edges, policy, ini, aliases, 
order="partner_id\trank\nP1\t1\n"):
+30|    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+31|    (folder / "schema_versions.json").write_text(json.dumps({"schemas": 
schemas}), encoding="utf-8")
+32|    (folder / "migration_edges.csv").write_text("\n".join(edges) + "\n", 
encoding="utf-8")
+33|    (folder / "field_policy.tsv").write_text("\n".join(policy) + "\n", 
encoding="utf-8")
+34|    (folder / "null_semantics.ini").write_text(ini, encoding="utf-8")
+35|    (folder / "partner_aliases.xml").write_text(aliases, encoding="utf-8")
+36|    (folder / "report_taxonomy.json").write_text(json.dumps(TAXONOMY), 
encoding="utf-8")
+37|    (folder / "partner_order.tsv").write_text(order, encoding="utf-8")
+38|    return folder
+39|
+40|
+41|SCHEMAS = [
+42|    {"id": "d.v1", "domain": "d", "canonical": False},
+43|    {"id": "d.v2", "domain": "d", "canonical": False},
+44|    {"id": "d.v3", "domain": "d", "canonical": True},
+45|]
+46|POLICY = [
+47|    "domain\tfield\tdefault\tfill\tnull_counts_as",
+48|    "d\ta\t1\tabsent_or_null\t",
+49|    "d\tb\t\t\tabsent",
+50|    "d\tc\t\"x\"\t\tpresent",
+51|    "d\tz\t2\t\t",
+52|]
+53|INI = (
+54|    "[presence]\nexplicit_null = present\n\n[fill]\nmode = absent_only\n\n"
+55|    "[tokens]\ndefault = NULL | N/A\nP2 = -\nP3 =\n"
+56|)
+57|ALIASES = (
+58|    '<partners>\n'
+59|    '  <partner id="P1"><alias>one</alias></partner>\n'
+60|    '  <partner id="P2"><alias>two</alias></partner>\n'
+61|    '  <partner id="P3"><alias>three</alias></partner>\n'
+62|    '  <partner id="P4"/><partner id="P5"/><partner id="P6"/><partner 
id="P7"/>\n'
+63|    '  <merge from="P4" into="P5"/><merge from="P5" into="P1"/>\n'
+64|    '  <merge from="P6" into="P7"/><merge from="P7" into="P6"/>\n'
+65|    '</partners>\n'
+66|)

+69|class Checks(unittest.TestCase):
+70|    def setUp(self):
+71|        self.scratch = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="migrakit-checks-"))
+72|
+73|    def contracts(self, edges, schemas=SCHEMAS, policy=POLICY, ini=INI, 
aliases=ALIASES, order="partner_id\trank\nP2\t5\nP1\t9\n"):
+74|        folder = write(self.scratch / 
f"c{len(list(self.scratch.iterdir()))}", schemas, edges, policy, ini, aliases, 
order)
+75|        return migrakit.load_contracts(folder)

+77|    # ---- partner identity (P rules) -------------------------------------
+78|    def test_merge_chain(self):
+79|        reg = 
self.contracts(["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost"])
+80|        self.assertEqual(migrakit.resolve_partner(reg, "P4"), "P1")
+81|        self.assertEqual(migrakit.resolve_partner(reg, "one"), "P1")
+82|
+83|    def test_alias_cycle(self):
+84|        reg = 
self.contracts(["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost"])
+85|        with self.assertRaises(migrakit.MigrationError) as caught:
+86|            migrakit.resolve_partner(reg, "P6")
+87|        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "E_ALIAS_CYCLE")
+88|
+89|    def test_unknown_partner(self):
+90|        reg = 
self.contracts(["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost"])
+91|        with self.assertRaises(migrakit.MigrationError) as caught:
+92|            migrakit.resolve_partner(reg, "nobody")
+93|        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER")

+95|    # ---- state signature (M10, N1) --------------------------------------
+96|    def test_signature_keeps_null(self):
+97|        reg = self.contracts([
+98|            "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
+99|            "A1,d.v1,d.v2,has:a;lacks:b,,1",
+100|            "A2,d.v2,d.v3,,,1",
+101|        ])
+102|        payload = {"a": None, "q": 1}
+103|        self.assertEqual(migrakit.state_signature(reg, "d.v1", payload), 
{"a": "null", "b": "absent"})
+104|        self.assertEqual(payload, {"a": None, "q": 1})
+105|

+109|            migrakit.state_signature(reg, "d.v9", {})

+112|    # ---- planning (M6 to M9) --------------------------------------------
+113|    def test_plan_revisits_a_schema(self):
+114|        reg = self.contracts([
+115|            "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
+116|            "A1,d.v1,d.v2,,drop:b,1",
+117|            "A2,d.v2,d.v1,,,1",
+118|            "A3,d.v1,d.v3,lacks:b,,1",
+119|        ])
+120|        self.assertEqual(migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {"b": 1}), 
["A1", "A2", "A3"])
+121|
+122|    def test_plan_prefers_cheapest_then_smallest_list(self):
+123|        reg = self.contracts([
+124|            "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
+125|            "B1,d.v1,d.v3,,,5",
+126|            "A9,d.v1,d.v2,,,2",
+127|            "A8,d.v2,d.v3,,,3",
+128|            "A7,d.v1,d.v2,,,2",
+129|        ])
+130|        self.assertEqual(migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {}), ["A7", 
"A8"])

+132|    def test_plan_unreachable(self):

+138|            migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {})

+141|    def test_plan_sees_payload_as_given(self):
+142|        reg = self.contracts([
+143|            "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
+144|            "A1,d.v1,d.v3,has:b,,1",
+145|        ])
+146|        # b is an explicit null and policy says it counts as absent for 
domain d
+147|        with self.assertRaises(migrakit.MigrationError):
+148|            migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {"b": None})
+149|        # c counts as present when null
+150|        reg2 = self.contracts([
+151|            "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
+152|            "A1,d.v1,d.v3,has:c,,1",
+153|        ])
+154|        self.assertEqual(migrakit.plan_migration(reg2, "d.v1", {"c": 
None}), ["A1"])

+163|    # ---- operations (M4, M5, N4, N5) ------------------------------------
+164|    def test_fill_modes_and_rename_of_null(self):
+165|        reg = self.contracts([
+166|            "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
+167|            "A1,d.v1,d.v2,,add:a;add:z,1",
+168|            "A2,d.v2,d.v3,,rename:a>b,1",
+169|        ])
+170|        out = migrakit.execute_plan(reg, "d.v1", {"a": None, "z": None}, 
["A1", "A2"], [])
+171|        # a: fill absent_or_null -> default 1, then renamed to b; z: 
absent_only -> stays null
+172|        self.assertEqual(out["payload"], {"b": 1, "z": None})

+182|    # ---- execution (M11, M12, C rules) ----------------------------------
+183|    def test_corrections_pinned_and_prereq_failure(self):
+184|        reg = self.contracts([
+185|            "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
+186|            "A1,d.v1,d.v2,,,1",
+187|            "A2,d.v2,d.v3,has:b,,1",
+188|        ])
+189|        plan = migrakit.plan_migration(reg, "d.v1", {"b": 1})
+190|        self.assertEqual(plan, ["A1", "A2"])
+191|        payload = {"b": 1}
+192|        out = migrakit.execute_plan(
+193|            reg, "d.v1", payload, plan,
+194|            [{"id": "C1", "op": "UNSET", "schema": "d.v2", "field": "b", 
"value": None}],
+195|        )
+196|        self.assertEqual(
+197|            (out["status"], out["code"], out["schema"], 
out["completed_steps"], out["failed_edge"], out["payload"]),
+198|            ("failed", "E_PREREQ_FAILED", "d.v2", 1, "A2", None),
+199|        )
+200|        self.assertEqual(out["corrections_applied"], ["C1"])
+201|        self.assertEqual(payload, {"b": 1})
+202|
+203|    def test_corrections_apply_on_first_arrival_only(self):
+204|        reg = self.contracts([
+205|            "edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",
+206|            "A1,d.v1,d.v2,,,1",
+207|            "A2,d.v2,d.v1,,,1",
+208|            "A3,d.v1,d.v3,has:m,,1",
+209|        ])
+210|        corrections = [
+211|            {"id": "C1", "op": "SET", "schema": "d.v1", "field": "m", 
"value": 7},
+212|            {"id": "C2", "op": "SET", "schema": "d.v2", "field": "n", 
"value": 8},
+213|            {"id": "C3", "op": "SET", "schema": "d.v1", "field": "m", 
"value": 9},
+214|        ]
+215|        out = migrakit.execute_plan(reg, "d.v1", {}, ["A1", "A2", "A3"], 
corrections)
+216|        self.assertEqual(out["status"], "migrated")
+217|        self.assertEqual(out["payload"], {"m": 9, "n": 8})
+218|        self.assertEqual(out["corrections_applied"], ["C1", "C3", "C2"])

+232|    # ---- pipeline (K13 to K18) ------------------------------------------
+233|    def run_feed(self, edges, feed_lines, corrections, manifest, **kwargs):
+234|        folder = write(
+235|            self.scratch / f"c{len(list(self.scratch.iterdir()))}", 
SCHEMAS, edges, POLICY, INI, ALIASES,
+236|            kwargs.get("order", "partner_id\trank\nP2\t5\nP1\t9\n"),
+237|        )
+238|        feed = folder / "feed.jsonl"
+239|        feed.write_text("".join(json.dumps(line) + "\n" if isinstance(line,
dict) else line + "\n" for line in feed_lines), encoding="utf-8")
+240|        (folder / "corr.txt").write_text(corrections, encoding="utf-8")
+241|        (folder / "man.csv").write_text(manifest, encoding="utf-8")
+242|        return migrakit.run_pipeline(folder, feed, folder / "corr.txt", 
folder / "man.csv")

+244|    def test_processing_order_and_replay(self):
+245|        edges = ["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost", 
"A1,d.v1,d.v3,,,1"]
+246|        # line 2 belongs to the batch received first, so it holds the 
identity
+247|        feed = [
+248|            {"batch": "B2", "partner": "one", "key": "k", "schema": "d.v1",
"payload": {"v": 1}},
+249|            {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P1", "key": "k", "schema": "d.v1", 
"payload": {"v": 2}},
+250|        ]
+251|        fp1 = migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": 1})
+252|        fp2 = migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": 2})
+253|        manifest = (
+254|            "batch_id,received_seq,lines,fp_xor\n"
+255|            f"B1,1,1,{fp2[:8]}\nB2,2,1,{fp1[:8]}\n"
+256|        )
+257|        records, summary = self.run_feed(edges, feed, "# none\n", manifest)
+258|        self.assertEqual([r["status"] for r in records], ["rejected", 
"migrated"])
+259|        self.assertEqual(records[0]["code"], "E_REPLAY_CONFLICT")
+260|        self.assertEqual(summary["receipt_mismatches"], [])
+261|        # the reissue of the later-processed line supersedes the holder
+262|        records, summary = self.run_feed(edges, feed, f"X1 REISSUE P1 k 
{fp1[:12]}\n", manifest)
+263|        self.assertEqual([r["status"] for r in records], ["migrated", 
"superseded"])
+264|        self.assertEqual(records[1]["payload"], None)
+265|        self.assertEqual(records[1]["code"], None)
+266|        self.assertEqual(summary["by_category"]["duplicate"], 1)
+267|        self.assertEqual(summary["invalid_corrections"], [])
+268|
+269|    def test_parse_and_receipts_and_partner_order(self):
+270|        edges = ["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost",

"payload": {}},
+277|        ]

+281|        self.assertEqual([r["code"] for r in records], ["E_PARSE", None,

+282|        self.assertEqual(records[0]["batch"], None)
+283|        self.assertEqual(records[2]["batch"], None)
+284|        self.assertEqual(records[3]["fingerprint"], None)
+285|        self.assertEqual(summary["receipt_mismatches"], ["B1"])

+288|            "complete": 0, "duplicate": 0, "execution_failure": 0,

+292|    def test_tokens_per_partner(self):
+293|        edges = ["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost", 
"A1,d.v1,d.v3,lacks:v,,1"]
+294|        feed = [
+295|            {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P2", "key": "k1", "schema": "d.v1",
"payload": {"v": "-"}},
+296|            {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P1", "key": "k2", "schema": "d.v1",
"payload": {"v": "-"}},
+297|            {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P3", "key": "k3", "schema": "d.v1",
"payload": {"v": "NULL"}},
+298|            {"batch": "B1", "partner": "P1", "key": "k4", "schema": "d.v1",
"payload": {"v": " NULL "}},
+299|        ]
+300|        manifest = "batch_id,received_seq,lines,fp_xor\nB1,1,4,%s\n" % 
"%08x" % (
+301|            int(migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": "-"})[:8], 16)
+302|            ^ int(migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": "-"})[:8], 16)
+303|            ^ int(migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": "NULL"})[:8], 
16)
+304|            ^ int(migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {"v": " NULL "})[:8],
16)
+305|        )
+306|        records, summary = self.run_feed(edges, feed, "# none\n", manifest)
+307|        # P2's own token line replaces the default, P3's line is empty, and
tokens match exactly
+308|        self.assertEqual([r["status"] for r in records], ["rejected", 
"migrated", "rejected", "migrated"])
+309|        self.assertEqual(records[1]["payload"], {"v": None})
+310|        self.assertEqual(summary["receipt_mismatches"], [])
+311|
+312|    def test_invalid_corrections(self):
+313|        edges = ["edge_id,source,target,requires,operations,cost", 
"A1,d.v1,d.v3,,,1"]
+314|        feed = [{"batch": "B1", "partner": "P1", "key": "k", "schema": 
"d.v1", "payload": {}}]
+315|        manifest = "batch_id,received_seq,lines,fp_xor\nB1,1,1,%s\n" % 
migrakit.record_fingerprint("d.v1", {})[:8]
+316|        log = (
+317|            "# comment\n\n"
+318|            "X1 SET P1 k AT d.v1 w 5\n"
+319|            "X2 SET nobody k AT d.v1 w 5\n"
+320|            "X3 SET P1 other AT d.v1 w 5\n"
+321|            "X4 SET P1 k AT d.v2 w 5\n"
+322|            "X5 BOGUS P1 k\n"

+324|        )
+325|        records, summary = self.run_feed(edges, feed, log, manifest)
+326|        self.assertEqual(records[0]["payload"], {"w": 5})
+327|        self.assertEqual(summary["invalid_corrections"], ["X2", "X3", "X4",
"X5", "X6"])
+328|        self.assertEqual(summary["records"], 1)

+336|        env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(ROOT / "src"))

Reasoning:
All 20 checks pass. I'm double-checking some subtler contract details, like how

migrated, failed, and superseded cases - confirming the schema stays canonical 
when migration succeeded.

as does the batch-missing-from-manifest case landing correctly as E_PARSE. The 
partner summary structure, build_summary's default argument, state_signature's 
return type, and execute_plan's behavior with empty plans or invalid paths all 
line up with the spec. Now I'm reconsidering whether fingerprint should work a 
certain way.

Now I'm shifting to the deliverables list: packaging patched-repo.zip with clean
paths and no cache directories, writing summary.json as sorted, indented JSON, 
producing the per-module changed_functions reports via AST diffing, running 
visible tests for both repos, and assembling the repair report. I'm starting to 
write the AST diff logic, defining the rule that a function counts as changed if
its syntax tree differs from the shipped version once leading docstrings are 
stripped out.

I'm working out how to name functions and methods consistently — top-level 
functions as module.func, class methods as module.Class.method, with nested 
functions folded into whichever enclosing function or method contains them. 
Module paths need to map relative to the migrakit package, so I'm walking each 
file's AST, pulling out FunctionDef/AsyncFunctionDef and ClassDef nodes, 
stripping the leading docstring from each function body, and comparing the 
resulting trees with ast.dump to detect real changes.

Now I'm moving on to writing the actual script and generating the module files.



Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/tmp/astdiff.py"
  file_text: 
    """Compare the shipped and the repaired package function by function."""
    
    import ast
    import json
    from pathlib import Path
    
    OLD = Path("/app/orig-repo/src/migrakit")
    NEW = Path("/app/repo/src/migrakit")
    
    
    def _strip_doc(node):
        body = list(node.body)
        if body and isinstance(body[0], ast.Expr) and isinstance(body[0].value, 
ast.Constant) and isinstance(body[0].value.value, str):
            body = body[1:]
        clone = ast.parse("def _():\n    pass").body[0]
        clone.body = body or [ast.Pass()]
        clone.name = node.name
        clone.args = node.args
        clone.decorator_list = node.decorator_list
        clone.returns = node.returns
        return ast.dump(clone)
    
    
    def functions(path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
        tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        found = {}
        for node in tree.body:
            if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
                found[node.name] = _strip_doc(node)
            elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
                for item in node.body:
                    if isinstance(item, (ast.FunctionDef, 
ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
                        found[f"{node.name}.{item.name}"] = _strip_doc(item)
        return found
    
    
    def module_name(path: Path, root: Path) -> str:
        rel = path.relative_to(root).with_suffix("")
        return ".".join(rel.parts)
    
    
    def area(module: str) -> str | None:
        if module.startswith("core.") or module == "pipeline":
            return "core"
        if module.startswith("parser.") or module == "cli":
            return "parser"
        if module.startswith("report."):
            return "report"
        return None
    
    
    def main() -> None:
        changed: dict[str, list[str]] = {"core": [], "parser": [], "report": []}
        for new_path in sorted(NEW.rglob("*.py")):
            if "__pycache__" in new_path.parts:
                continue
            module = module_name(new_path, NEW)
            where = area(module)
            if where is None:
                continue
            old_path = OLD / new_path.relative_to(NEW)
            old = functions(old_path) if old_path.exists() else {}
            for name, tree in functions(new_path).items():
                if old.get(name) != tree:
                    changed[where].append(f"{module}.{name}")
        print(json.dumps({key: sorted(value) for key, value in changed.items()},
indent=2))
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

reasoning 1.23K (total 37.93K) • ↓ output 2.29K (total 76.09K) • $ (total 
5.1194)

    """Run tests/test_visible.py of one repository and report pass/fail per test
method."""
    
    import json
    import sys
    import unittest
    from pathlib import Path
    
    root = Path(sys.argv[1])
    sys.path.insert(0, str(root / "src"))
    sys.path.insert(0, str(root / "tests"))
    
    loader = unittest.TestLoader()
    suite = loader.discover(str(root / "tests"), pattern="test_visible.py")
    
    result = {}
    for group in suite:
        for case in group:
            for test in case:
                outcome = unittest.TextTestRunner(stream=open("/dev/null", "w"),
verbosity=0).run(unittest.TestSuite([test]))
                name = test.id().rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
                result[name] = "pass" if outcome.wasSuccessful() else "fail"
    
    print(json.dumps(result, sort_keys=True))

+    """Best plan from ``source`` to ``target``, searching over (schema, field 
state).
+
+    A label-setting search: the labels ``(cost, edge ids)`` are popped in M8 
order,
+    and because every edge cost is positive, a label that loses on one state 
can
+    never win once extended, so one label per state is enough. Schemas may 
repeat
+    on a plan, states may not.
+    """
+    best: dict[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], tuple[int, list[str]]] = {(source, 
state): (0, [])}
