    """Build the /app/out deliverables."""
    
    import json
    import subprocess
    import sys
    import zipfile
    from pathlib import Path
    
    REPO = Path("/app/repo")
    OUT = Path("/app/out")
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "src"))
    sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp")
    import migrakit  # noqa: E402
    from astdiff import NEW, area, functions, module_name  # noqa: E402
    
    # ---- patched-repo.zip -------------------------------------------------
    members = [REPO / "README.md", REPO / "pyproject.toml", REPO / 
"tests/test_visible.py"]
    members += sorted(p for p in (REPO / "src").rglob("*") if p.is_file() and 
"__pycache__" not in p.parts)
    archive = OUT / "patched-repo.zip"
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for path in members:
            zf.write(path, str(path.relative_to(REPO)))
    print("archive members:", len(members))
    
    # ---- summary.json -----------------------------------------------------
    records, summary = migrakit.run_pipeline(
        "/app/ci", "/app/ci/partner_batches.jsonl", 
"/app/ci/correction_log.txt", "/app/ci/replay_manifest.csv"
    )
    (OUT / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, 
sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    
    # ---- module_*.json ----------------------------------------------------
    changed: dict[str, list[str]] = {"core": [], "parser": [], "report": []}
    for new_path in sorted(NEW.rglob("*.py")):
        if "__pycache__" in new_path.parts:
            continue
        module = module_name(new_path, NEW)
        where = area(module)
        if where is None:
            continue
        old_path = Path("/app/orig-repo/src/migrakit") / 
new_path.relative_to(NEW)
        old = functions(old_path) if old_path.exists() else {}
        for name, tree in functions(new_path).items():
            if old.get(name) != tree:
                changed[where].append(f"{module}.{name}")
    
    NOTES = {
        "core": [
            "core.records.FieldView.from_payload kept an explicit null out of 
the state map, so a null field looked absent (N1); it now maps null to the null 
state.",

            "core.planner._enumerate_paths checked every prerequisite on the 
record as received, refused to revisit a schema and enumerated paths one by one;
it is now a label-setting search over (schema, field state) that checks 
prerequisites on the state reached at that point of the plan (M6), may pass

astronomically many paths (K14).",

at their pinned schema (C4, new helper core.executor._arrive) and a failing 
prerequisite stops the run with status failed, code E_PREREQ_FAILED, the edge 
that failed and the steps completed (M11).",

            "pipeline._Run._admit keyed the replay identity by the partner as

        ],
        "parser": [

import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET, collections
root=ET.parse('partner_aliases.xml').getroot()
al=collections.Counter(); pid=set()
for p in root.iter('partner'):
    pid.add(p.get('id'))
    for a in p.iter('alias'): al[(a.text or '').strip()]+=1
print('partners',len(pid),'aliases',len(al))
print('dup aliases',[k for k,v in al.items() if v>1])
print('alias equal to a partner id', [k for k in al if k in pid])
m=collections.Counter(e.get('from') for e in root.iter('merge'))
print('dup merge from',[k for k,v in m.items() if v>1])
print('merge targets not partners', [e.get('into') for e in root.iter('merge') 
if e.get('into') not in pid][:5])

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/tmp/make_report.py"
  file_text: 
    """Run the acceptance checks and write /app/out/repair-report.json."""
    
    import copy
    import json
    import subprocess
    import sys
    import time
    import zipfile
    from pathlib import Path
    
    REPO = Path("/app/repo")
    OUT = Path("/app/out")
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "src"))
    import migrakit  # noqa: E402
    
    CI = ("/app/ci", "/app/ci/partner_batches.jsonl", 
"/app/ci/correction_log.txt", "/app/ci/replay_manifest.csv")
    checks = []
    
    
    def check(name, fn):
        try:
            passed = bool(fn())
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            print(name, "raised", exc)
            passed = False
        checks.append({"name": name, "passed": passed})
        print(("PASS " if passed else "FAIL "), name)
    
    
    def visible_tests():
        done = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-m", "unittest", "discover", "-s", "tests"],
            cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, text=True, env={"PYTHONPATH": 
str(REPO / "src"), "PATH": "/usr/bin:/bin"},
        )
        return done.returncode == 0 and "Ran 9 tests" in done.stderr
    
    
    def contract_checks():
        done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "/tmp/checks.py"], 
capture_output=True, text=True)
        return done.returncode == 0
    
    
    def planner_matches_brute_force():
        done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "/tmp/brute.py"], 
capture_output=True, text=True)
        print(done.stdout.strip()[-80:])
        return done.returncode == 0
    
    
    def receipts_match():
        _, summary = migrakit.run_pipeline(*CI)
        return summary["receipt_mismatches"] == []
    
    
    def planner_is_fast():
        registry = migrakit.load_contracts("/tmp/stress/c2")
        worst = 0.0
        for index in range(1, 201):
            start = time.perf_counter()
            try:
                migrakit.plan_migration(registry, f"big.v{index}", {"f1": 1, 
"f3": None})
            except migrakit.MigrationError:
                pass
            worst = max(worst, time.perf_counter() - start)
        print(f"  worst plan_migration call: {worst:.3f}s")
        return worst < 10.0
    
    
    def archive_layout():
        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUT / "patched-repo.zip") as archive:
            names = archive.namelist()
        expected = {"README.md", "pyproject.toml", "tests/test_visible.py", 
"src/migrakit/__init__.py", "src/migrakit/__main__.py"}
        return (
            expected <= set(names)
            and all(not name.endswith("/") for name in names)
            and all(not name.startswith("/") and ".." not in name.split("/") for
name in names)
        )
    
    
    def arguments_unchanged():
        registry = migrakit.load_contracts("/app/ci")
        with open("/app/ci/partner_batches.jsonl", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            lines = [json.loads(line) for line in handle if 
line.startswith("{")]
        for document in lines[:400]:
            schema, payload = document.get("schema"), document.get("payload")
            if not isinstance(schema, str) or schema not in registry.schemas or 
not isinstance(payload, dict):
                continue
            keep = copy.deepcopy(payload)
            migrakit.state_signature(registry, schema, payload)
            try:
                plan = migrakit.plan_migration(registry, schema, payload)
            except migrakit.MigrationError:
                continue
            corrections = [{"id": "C", "op": "SET", "schema": schema, "field": 
"probe", "value": [1, {"x": 2}]}]
            frozen = copy.deepcopy(corrections)
            migrakit.execute_plan(registry, schema, payload, plan, corrections)
            if payload != keep or corrections != frozen:
                return False
        return True
    
    
    def cli_matches_run_pipeline():
        out = Path("/tmp/clicheck")
        done = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-m", "migrakit", "run", "--contracts", CI[0], 
"--feed", CI[1],
             "--corrections", CI[2], "--manifest", CI[3], "--out", str(out)],
            cwd="/tmp", capture_output=True, text=True, env={"PYTHONPATH": 
str(REPO / "src"), "PATH": "/usr/bin:/bin"},
        )
        if done.returncode != 0:
            print(done.stderr)
            return False
        records, summary = migrakit.run_pipeline(*CI)
        written = [json.loads(line) for line in (out / 
"records.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
        return written == records and json.loads((out / 
"run_summary.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")) == summary
    
    
    def summary_file_matches():
        _, summary = migrakit.run_pipeline(*CI)
        return json.loads((OUT / "summary.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")) ==
summary
    
    
    check("visible tests pass on the repaired repository", visible_tests)
    check("contract checks on synthetic contract folders pass", contract_checks)
    check("plan_migration agrees with a brute-force search on shipped records", 
planner_matches_brute_force)
    check("every batch receipt of the shipped manifest matches (K13)", 
receipts_match)
    check("plan_migration stays well inside 10 s on a 200-schema, 3000-edge 
domain (K14)", planner_is_fast)
    check("patched-repo.zip has the K1 layout, no directory entries, no absolute
or .. names", archive_layout)
    check("state_signature, plan_migration and execute_plan leave their 
arguments unchanged (K7)", arguments_unchanged)
    check("python -m migrakit run writes the records and summary of run_pipeline
(K10)", cli_matches_run_pipeline)
    check("out/summary.json is the summary of the shipped inputs", 
summary_file_matches)
    
    FIXES = [
        "core/records.py: FieldView.from_payload dropped explicit nulls, so a 
null field was indistinguishable from an absent one; it now records the null 
state (N1).",
        "core/records.py: null_counts_as treated an empty null_counts_as column 
as 'absent'; it now falls back to [presence] explicit_null of null_semantics.ini
(N2).",
        "core/records.py: fill_mode ignored the fill column of field_policy.tsv;
the column now wins over [fill] mode (N3, N4).",
        "core/planner.py: the planner judged every prerequisite on the record as
received; prerequisites are now judged on the record state reached at that point
of the plan (M6).",
        "core/planner.py: the planner refused to enter a schema twice; a plan 
may now pass through a schema more than once (M7).",
        "core/planner.py: a cost tie was broken by plan length; it is now broken
by comparing the lists of edge ids element by element (M8).",
        "core/planner.py: path enumeration was exponential; a label-setting 
search over (schema, field state) now answers within the K14 budget on graphs 
with astronomically many paths.",
        "core/executor.py: every correction was applied before the first edge; a
correction is now applied on the first arrival at its pinned schema, in log

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Brute-force planner comparison script

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/tmp/brute.py"
  file_text: 
    """Compare plan_migration with a depth-limited brute-force search over the 
shipped feed."""
    
    import json
    import random
    import sys
    
    sys.path.insert(0, "/app/repo/src")
    import migrakit
    from migrakit.core.operations import apply_operations
    from migrakit.core.planner import _stand_in
    from migrakit.core.records import FieldView, edge_allowed
    
    reg = migrakit.load_contracts("/app/ci")
    DEPTH = 7
    
    
    def brute(schema, payload):
        domain = reg.schemas[schema]
        target = reg.canonical[domain]
        names = reg.named_fields(domain)
        best = [None]
    
        def walk(node, state, path, cost, depth):
            if node == target:
                candidate = (cost, list(path))
                if best[0] is None or candidate < best[0]:
                    best[0] = candidate
                return
            if depth == 0:
                return
            view = FieldView(dict(zip(names, state)))
            for edge in reg.edges_from.get(node, ()):
                if not edge_allowed(reg, domain, view, edge):
                    continue
                record = apply_operations(reg, domain, _stand_in(names, state), 
edge.operations)
                path.append(edge.edge_id)
                walk(edge.target, FieldView.from_payload(record).states(names), 
path, cost + edge.cost, depth - 1)
                path.pop()
    
        walk(schema, FieldView.from_payload(payload).states(names), [], 0, 
DEPTH)
        return best[0][1] if best[0] else None
    
    
    feed = [json.loads(line) for line in open("/app/ci/partner_batches.jsonl", 
encoding="utf-8") if line.startswith("{")]
    random.seed(11)
    checked = bad = 0
    for document in random.sample(feed, 200):
        schema, payload = document.get("schema"), document.get("payload")
        if not isinstance(schema, str) or schema not in reg.schemas or not 
isinstance(payload, dict):
            continue
        tokens = reg.tokens_for(migrakit.resolve_partner(reg, 
document["partner"]) if document["partner"] in reg.partners else "?")
        payload = {name: (None if isinstance(value, str) and value in tokens 
else value) for name, value in payload.items()}
        try:
            got = migrakit.plan_migration(reg, schema, payload)
        except migrakit.MigrationError:
            got = None
        want = brute(schema, payload)
        checked += 1
        if got != want and not (want is None and got is not None and len(got) > 
DEPTH):
            bad += 1
            print("MISMATCH", schema, got, want)
    print(f"compared {checked} records, {bad} mismatches")
    sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

assignment for rejected records.

batches in E_PARSE — checking that each resolves consistently with the contract.
Now I want to check one more detail about how run_pipeline handles items not 
found in `received`.

Items with a batch present in the manifest but a non-integer received_seq would 
cause int() to raise, which seems fine; duplicate received_seq values across 
batches get tied broken by line order since the sort key includes item.line, so 
that's stable. I also verify that summary["partners"] includes partners from 
records at any status, including rejected ones with non-null partner values, and
that the same identity appearing across different batches with matching 
fingerprints is correctly flagged as replayed.

Now I'm doing a final consistency check on the record dictionaries: confirming 
all 13 keys — line, batch, partner, key, status, code, schema, plan, 
completed_steps, failed_edge, payload, attempted, fingerprint — are present in 
every record and match what K17 expects, along with checking that status/code 
combinations are all valid.

Summary: Repaired migrakit and wrote all deliverables

Finish with message:
I repaired `migrakit` so every stage follows the contract, and wrote all 
deliverables to `/app/out`.

**Defects found and fixed (19 code fixes across 9 files + one missing file)**

core (`core/`, `pipeline.py`)
- `FieldView.from_payload` dropped explicit nulls, so a null field looked absent
(N1).
- `null_counts_as` used a hard-coded "absent" fallback (and a misleading README 
note) instead of `[presence] explicit_null` (N2).
- `fill_mode` ignored the `fill` column of `field_policy.tsv` (N3/N4).
- Planner: prerequisites were judged on the record *as received* instead of the 
state reached at that point of the plan (M6); it refused to re-enter a schema 
(M7); ties were broken by plan length instead of the edge-id list (M8); and it 
enumerated paths exponentially. It is now a label-setting search over (schema, 
field state) — worst observed call 0.05 s on a 200-schema/3000-edge/10-field 
domain with astronomically many paths (K14).
- Executor: applied all corrections up front instead of on first arrival at the 
pinned schema in log order (C4), and never checked prerequisites — 
E_PREREQ_FAILED with `failed_edge`, `completed_steps`, schema reached and null 
payload now works (M11/M12).
- Pipeline: feed order instead of `received_seq`-then-line order (K13); replay 
identity keyed by the raw partner, not the canonical id (K16/P4); only executed 
records could hold an identity; null tokens matched after trimming instead of 
exactly (N6); the batch-receipt XOR skipped records rejected after the partner 
stage (K13).

parser (`parser/`, `cli.py`)
- `resolve_partner` followed only one merge link and never detected a cycle 
(P2/P3, E_ALIAS_CYCLE).
- Partner token lines were merged with the defaults, and an empty partner line 
fell back to the defaults (N7).
- A non-JSON feed line crashed the run instead of being an E_PARSE record (K15).
- CLI resolved relative paths against the package folder (K10), and 
`src/migrakit/__main__.py` was missing entirely, so `python -m migrakit` could 
not run.

report (`report/`)
- `classify_result` reported "migrated" for every record that had a plan.
- `build_summary` used statuses as category names (now mapped through the 
taxonomy, every category listed, T1/T2) and sorted partners by id instead of by 
`partner_order.tsv` rank (T3).

**Verification** — the 9 visible tests are unchanged and all pass (2 failed 
before); 20 additional contract checks on synthetic contract folders pass (merge
chains/cycles, null states and fill modes, casts, schema-revisiting plans, M8 
tie-break, pinned-correction arrival and prerequisite failure, token rules, 
processing order/replay/reissue/supersede, receipts, parse errors, taxonomy and 
partner order, CLI); `plan_migration` matches a brute-force search on 200 
shipped records; all 2,200 batch receipts of the shipped manifest match; public 
functions leave their arguments unchanged; the CLI output equals 
`run_pipeline`'s.

**Shipped-input run**: 8000 records — migrated 5604, rejected 1969 
(E_UNREACHABLE 1787, E_UNKNOWN_SCHEMA 74, E_REPLAY_CONFLICT 71, 
E_UNKNOWN_PARTNER 25, E_PARSE 12), replayed 383, superseded 44; 405 invalid 
corrections; no receipt mismatches.

**Written**: `patched-repo.zip` (22 members, K1 layout at the root, no 
directory/absolute/`..` names), `summary.json`, `module_core.json`, 
`module_parser.json`, `module_report.json`, `test_fixes.json`, 
`repair-report.json` (22 fixes, 9/9 checks passed). Only the Python standard 
library is used.
